&\leqslant\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\widetilde{\phi}_{\hat{\rho},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}\\
&\leqslant\frac{1}{|\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\widetilde{\phi}_{\hat{\rho},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}\\
&\qquad+\lambda 3^{-(m-n)}|p|^{2}.\end{split}

Here the last error term
\lambda 3^{-(m-n)}|p|^{2}
comes from the calculation of Dirichlet energy in the boundary layer of width
3^{n}
, where we do not pose any corrector and the affine function can be calculated directly. For the Dirichlet energy in the interior and when
3^{n}>\mathbf{r}
, then for every
z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}
, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\widetilde{\phi}_{\hat{\rho},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}))^{2}\in\mathcal{F}_{0}({z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n+1}}),

and
z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n+1}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}
. Then Lemma
6.5
-(1) applies to this case (
z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},{\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}>\mathbf{r}}
) and we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\widetilde{\phi}_{\hat{\rho},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{(1+3^{d(2n-m)})}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\widetilde{\phi}_{\hat{\rho},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\hat{\rho}}

\displaystyle\leqslant(1+3^{d(2n-m)})\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot{\overbracket{D}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})\xi\right).

We put this back to (
6.28
) and concludes that

(6.29)

\displaystyle\widehat{D}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\leqslant{\overbracket{D}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})+C(\lambda)(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id}.

Step 2: comparison between
\widehat{D}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)
and
{\overbracket{D}}_{*}(\hat{\rho},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
.
This part is quite close to that in Step 1.
We make the decomposition that

(6.30)

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}}\left\langle{(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})(\pi_{b}v)-\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}\\
&=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})(\pi_{b}v)-\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}},\\
\text{other bonds}\end{subarray}}\left\langle{(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})(\pi_{b}v)-\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}\end{split}

Let
u_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho},q}
be the maximizer for the problem
\overline{\nu}_{*}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho},q)
. Then for the case
z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},{\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}>\mathbf{r}}
and by the definition of
\overline{\nu}_{*}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho},q)
, we also have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})(\pi_{b}v)-\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{1}{2\chi(\hat{\rho})|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})(\pi_{b}u_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho},q})-\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}u_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho},q})^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M}

\displaystyle\leqslant(1+3^{d(2n-m)})\left(\frac{1}{2}q\cdot{\overbracket{D}}_{*}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})q\right).

Here from the first line to the second line, we use the fact that
u_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho},q}
is also the maixmiser under the canonical ensemble and gives positive functional; see (
4.11
) and Remark
4.2
for details. Then from the second line to the third line, note the positivity of the integral, Lemma
6.5
-(1) applies. We only needs to treat the boundary layer and the layer between the small cubes appearing in the third line of (
6.30
), which can be solved by the following uniform point-wise estimate that

\displaystyle(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})(\pi_{b}v)-\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}\leqslant\frac{1}{2}(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}(\pi_{b}v)^{2}=\frac{1}{2}(\pi_{b}\ell_{q})^{2}.

Therefore, (
6.30
) can be reduced to

\displaystyle\widehat{D}_{*}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\leqslant{\overbracket{D}}_{*}^{-1}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})+C(\lambda)(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id},

which implies, noting
{\overbracket{D}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})\leqslant\lambda\mathsf{Id}
, that

(6.31)

\displaystyle\widehat{D}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\geqslant{\overbracket{D}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})-C(\lambda)\lambda^{2}(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id}.

Step 3: conclusion.
Combining (
6.26
), (
6.29
) and (
6.31
), we obtain

{\overbracket{D}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})-C(\lambda)(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id}\leqslant\widehat{D}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\\
\leqslant\widehat{D}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\leqslant{\overbracket{D}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})+C(\lambda)(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id}.

Applying (
6.15
) and (
5.2
), this is interpreted as

\overline{\mathbf{c}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})-C(\lambda)\chi(\hat{\rho})(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id}\leqslant\hat{\mathbf{c}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\\
\leqslant\hat{\mathbf{c}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)\leqslant\overline{\mathbf{c}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\hat{\rho})+C(\lambda)\chi(\hat{\rho})(3^{-(m-n)}+3^{-d(m-2n)})\mathsf{Id}.

Inserting the result of homogenization (
5.3
), and by a choice of
n
that

\displaystyle{d(m-2n)=\gamma_{1}n\Longleftrightarrow n=\frac{dm}{2d+\gamma_{1}}},

we obtain that

\displaystyle|\hat{\mathbf{c}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)-\overline{\mathbf{c}}(\hat{\rho})|+|\hat{\mathbf{c}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},M)-\overline{\mathbf{c}}(\hat{\rho})|\leqslant C3^{-\left(\frac{d\gamma_{1}}{2d+\gamma_{1}}\right)m}.

This is the desired result (
6.16
) by setting
\gamma_{2}:=\frac{d\gamma_{1}}{2d+\gamma_{1}}
.
∎

6.3.
Construction of density-free local corrector

This subsection is devoted to removing the dependence of density in the local corrector. A first natural idea is to consider the following variational problem for bounded set
\Lambda\subseteq{\mathbb{Z}^{d}}
and
\xi\in{\mathbb{R}^{d}}

(6.32)

\displaystyle\mu(\Lambda,\xi):=\inf_{v\in\mathcal{F}_{0}(\Lambda^{-})}\sup_{\rho\in[0,1]}\left\{\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{b\in\overline{\Lambda^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+v))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}-\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(\rho)\xi\right\}.

As expected, the uniform convergence can be improved as follows.

Proposition 6.6
.

There exists an exponent
\gamma_{3}(d,\lambda,\mathbf{r})>0
and a constant
{C(d,\lambda,\mathbf{r})<\infty}
such that for every
L\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\xi\in B_{1}
, we have

(6.33)

\displaystyle 0\leqslant\mu(\Lambda_{L},\xi)\leqslant CL^{-\gamma_{3}}.

Compared with (
4.1
), the variational problem (
6.32
) is more complicated. Therefore, instead of attacking (
6.32
) directly, we turn to construct
one
sub-minimizer with a good uniform convergence for
\rho\in[0,1]
. This is the main task in the remaining part of this subsection. We are inspired by the function used to prove the qualitative version of (
1.20
) in the previous work
[
34
, Lemma 2.1]
, where the main idea is to make the linear combination of the local corrector using the empirical density. Thus, we propose our density-free local corrector built on (
6.1
)

(6.34)

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\phi_{\overline{\eta}_{0}\vee\varepsilon\wedge(1-\varepsilon),z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}.

Recall
\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}=3^{n}{\mathbb{Z}^{d}}\cap{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}
. Here
\overline{\eta}_{0}
is a (random) empirical density defined as
{\overline{\eta}_{0}:=\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}}
. The mapping
\rho\mapsto\rho\vee\varepsilon\wedge(1-\varepsilon)
restricts the density away from
0
and
1
. That is, we make the truncation both for the spatial boundary layer and for the density, in order to avoid the perturbation from the rare but degenerate cases. More explicitly, recall the notation
\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}(\Lambda)
defined in (
6.17
), and denote by
M_{*},M^{*}

(6.35)

\displaystyle M_{*}:=\min\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}),\qquad M^{*}:=\max\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}),

then we can rewrite this corrector as

(6.36)

\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\phi_{\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right\}}.

When
n,\varepsilon
is well-chosen with respect to
m
, this corrector will give us uniform convergence under all grand canonical ensembles.

Proposition 6.7
.

There exist an exponent
\gamma_{4}(d,\lambda,\mathbf{r})>0
and a constant
{C(d,\lambda,\mathbf{r})<\infty}
such that for every
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, by setting
n:=\lfloor\frac{m}{9d+3}\rfloor
and
\varepsilon:=3^{-\frac{2dm}{9d+3}}
, we have

(6.37)

\displaystyle\sup_{\rho\in[0,1],\xi\in B_{1}}\left|\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\sum_{b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}}\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}-\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(\rho)\xi\right|\leqslant C3^{-\gamma_{4}m}.

Proof.

We assume
\mathbf{r}\ll 3^{n}\ll 3^{m}
and
0<\varepsilon\ll 1
with
n,\varepsilon
to be determined in the end. Since
\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}\in\mathcal{F}_{0}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})
, we compare it with the variational problem (
4.1
), which gives

(6.38)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\sum_{b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}}\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}\geqslant\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\xi\geqslant\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(\rho)\xi.

Thus it suffices to give the upper bound, and we can decompose the sum as

(6.39)

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}=\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III},\\
\mathbf{I}&:=\sum_{b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}},\dist(b,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho},\\
\mathbf{II}&:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}\setminus({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})^{*}\\
\dist(b,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\leqslant 3^{n}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho},\\
\mathbf{III}&:=\sum_{b\in({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})^{*}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}.\end{split}

Roughly,
\mathbf{I}
is the main contribution, while the terms
\mathbf{II}
and
\mathbf{III}
come from the boundary layer. Since the order of boundary layer is
3^{(d-1)m}
, they vanish after the normalization
\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}
when
m\nearrow\infty
. See Figure
3
for an illustration. We treat these three terms one by one in the remaining paragraphs.

Figure 3.
An illustration for the decomposition in (
6.39
): the bonds in the term
\mathbf{I}
,
\mathbf{II}
and
\mathbf{III}
are respectively marked in blue, green and red.

Step 1: term
\mathbf{I}
.
This is the case that the bonds stay in the interior, and is the main contribution of the Dirichlet energy. This term can be further decomposed as

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\leqslant\frac{1}{|\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}.

We focus on one term
(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
above and
b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}
, and have the following observation

(6.40)

\pi_{b}\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}(\eta)=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right\}}\pi_{b}\phi_{\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}(\eta),

because the Kawasaki operator
\pi_{b}
is conservative for the number of particles in
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}
and only one local corrector is perturbed. Therefore, we apply the decomposition of canonical ensemble for the sum over
\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}

\displaystyle=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{M,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}

\displaystyle\leqslant(1+4\cdot 3^{d(2n-m)})\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}.

We use the definition (
6.1
) that
\ell_{\xi}+\phi_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}=v\left(M/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)
from the first line to the second line. From the second line to the third line, we use the local equivalence of ensembles in (
6.18
) and need to assume

(6.41)

\displaystyle 1\leqslant n<\frac{m}{2}.

We then study the last line and distinguish it in
3
cases

Step 1.1: case
M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})
.
Then there is no bias between the probability space and the associated corrector, thus we have

(6.42)

\forall M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}),\quad\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}\\
=\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(M/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\xi\right).

Step 1.2: case
0\leqslant M\leqslant M_{*}
.
For this case, we have the following estimate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M_{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M_{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}.

Here from the second line to the third line, we use the regularity estimate (
6.7
), which gives an one-sided bias factor
\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}
. This factor will not explode because the targeted density
\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}
does not degenerate; recall the remark around (
6.5
). More precisely, using the definition of (
6.4
),
M\leqslant M_{*}
and
\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}\simeq\varepsilon\ll 1
, we have

\displaystyle\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}=\max\left\{\frac{M}{M_{*}},\frac{1-\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}{1-\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}\right\}=\frac{1-\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}{1-\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}\leqslant 1+\frac{\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}{1-\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}\leqslant 1+2\varepsilon,

which results in

\displaystyle\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\frac{M_{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}\leqslant(1+2\varepsilon)^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}\leqslant 1+10\cdot 3^{dn}\cdot\varepsilon.

Here we also need to assume

(6.43)

\displaystyle 0<\varepsilon\ll 3^{-dn}.

Using the stationarity, this concludes that

(6.44)

\forall 0\leqslant M<M_{*},\quad\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}\\
\leqslant\left(1+10\cdot 3^{dn}\cdot\varepsilon\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(M_{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\xi\right).

Step 1.3: case
M_{*}<M<|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|
.
This case is similar to Step 1.2, and we have

(6.45)

\forall M_{*}<M<|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,\quad\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{b\in\overline{{(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}^{*}}}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}\left(\pi_{b}v\left(M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi\right)\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}}\\
\leqslant\left(1+10\cdot 3^{dn}\cdot\varepsilon\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}(M^{*}/|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\xi\right).

Combing (
6.42
), (
6.44
), (
6.45
) and the spatial homogeneity from (2) of Hypothesis
1.1
, we obtain an estimate of the term
\mathbf{I}
under the assumption (
6.41
) and (
6.43
)

(6.46)

\mathbf{I}\leqslant\left(1+10\cdot 3^{d(2n-m)}+10\cdot 3^{dn}\cdot\varepsilon\right)\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\\
\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right).

We aim to compare this quantity with the target
\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi
, which relies on the concentration of measure and the regularity of
\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)
. We summarize this as Step 1.4.

Step 1.4: regularity on density.
Recall the Markov inequality of density

(6.47)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\left|\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}-\rho\right|>\delta\right]\leqslant\frac{\mathbb{V}\!\mathrm{ar}_{\rho}[\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}]}{\delta^{2}}=\frac{\rho(1-\rho)}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|\delta^{2}}.

We choose
\delta>0
in function of
\rho
and distinguish two cases. Viewing the assumption (
6.43
), here the threshold is tighter than
\varepsilon
.

Step 1.4.1: case
\rho\in[3^{-dn},1-3^{-dn}]
.
We choose the window
\delta:=3^{-3dn}
for such case, then treat the regime
\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta
at first in (
6.46
)

\displaystyle\sum_{M\in\mathbb{N},\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{M\in\mathbb{N},\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\rho}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}

\displaystyle\leqslant(1+3^{dn}\delta)^{2\cdot 3^{dn}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant(1+4\cdot 3^{-dn})\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right).

Here because of the assumption (
6.43
), we have
{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}=M}
for all
\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta
. Then we apply (
6.8
) from the first line to the second line. From the second line to the third line, we apply (
6.5
) to give an upper bound for
\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\rho}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}

\displaystyle\forall\rho\in[3^{-dn},1-3^{-dn}],\quad\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\frac{\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|}{\min\{\rho,1-\rho\}}<3^{dn}\delta

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq.RMKTheta}}}{{\Longrightarrow}}\Theta_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\rho}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}\leqslant(1+3^{dn}\delta)^{2\cdot 3^{dn}}.

Finally, we insert the choice
\delta=3^{-3dn}
from the third line to the forth line.

For the regime
\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\geqslant\delta
, we just use the Markov inequality (
6.47
) and the trivial bound of
\overline{\mathbf{c}}

\displaystyle\sum_{M\in\mathbb{N},\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\geqslant\delta}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)\leqslant\lambda 3^{d(6n-m)}.

Combing the two estimates above and (
6.46
), we conclude the estimate for
\mathbf{I}
under the condition
\rho\in[3^{-dn},1-3^{-dn}]

(6.48)

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\leqslant\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)+C(\lambda)\left(3^{dn}\varepsilon+3^{-dn}+3^{d(6n-m)}\right).

Step 1.4.2: case
\rho\in[0,3^{-dn})\cup(1-3^{-dn},1]
.
We choose the window
\delta:=3^{-\frac{dm}{2}}
, then for
{\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta}
, we have
\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}\in[0,2\cdot 3^{-dn}]\cup[1-2\cdot 3^{-dn},1]
since we assume (
6.41
). Then with the
a priori
bound
\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)\leqslant\chi(\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|})
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{M\in\mathbb{N},\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{M\in\mathbb{N},\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\leqslant\delta}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\chi\left(\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant C(\lambda)3^{-dn}.

For
{\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\geqslant\delta}
, we use Markov inequality (
6.47
) to give

\displaystyle\sum_{M\in\mathbb{N},\left|\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|}-\rho\right|\geqslant\delta}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\frac{M\vee M_{*}\wedge M^{*}}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)\leqslant C(\lambda)3^{-dn}.

Therefore, under (
6.41
) and (
6.43
), the estimate in (
6.46
) has an upper bound for
\rho\in[0,3^{-dn})\cup(1-3^{-dn},1]

(6.49)

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\leqslant C(\lambda)3^{-dn}.

Combing (
6.48
) and (
6.49
), we conclude the uniform estimate for Step 1 that

(6.50)

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}\leqslant\left(\frac{1}{2}\xi\cdot\overline{\mathbf{c}}\left(\rho,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}\right)\xi\right)+C(\lambda)\left(3^{dn}\varepsilon+3^{-dn}+3^{d(6n-m)}\right).

Step 2: term
\mathbf{II}
.
This term is the contribution of the boundary layer. For this case, because the local correctors and the indicator are both invariant when applying
\pi_{b}
, we have
\pi_{b}\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}(\eta)=0
. The only contribution comes from the affine function, which gives us

(6.51)

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}\leqslant\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\overline{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{*}}\setminus({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})^{*}\\
\dist(b,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\leqslant 3^{n}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}\ell_{\xi})^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}\leqslant\lambda\chi(\rho)3^{(n-m)}.

This term is small under the assumption (
6.41
).

Step 3: term
\mathbf{III}
.
This term is also the contribution of the boundary layer, but its estimate is more delicate. We denote by
b=\{y_{1},y_{2}\}
, where
y_{1}\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}
and
y_{2}\in\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}
. The operator
\pi_{y_{1},y_{2}}
makes difference only when
\eta_{y_{1}}\neq\eta_{y_{2}}
. For example, for the case
\eta_{y_{1}}=1
,
\eta_{y_{2}}=0
, we have

\displaystyle(\pi_{y_{1},y_{2}}\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi})(\eta)\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\eta_{y_{1}}=1,\eta_{y_{2}}=0\right\}}

\displaystyle=\left(\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}(\eta^{y_{1},y_{2}})-\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}(\eta)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\eta_{y_{1}}=1,\eta_{y_{2}}=0\right\}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\left(\phi_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}-\phi_{\frac{M+1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}\right)\right)\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-},x\neq y_{1}}\eta_{x}=M\right\}}\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\eta_{y_{1}}=1,\eta_{y_{2}}=0\right\}}.

Here we have
\phi_{\rho,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}(\eta^{y_{1},y_{2}})=\phi_{\rho,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}(\eta)
, because
y_{1},y_{2}
are both far from the support of the local function when
3^{n}\geqslant\mathbf{r}
. Meanwhile, the operator
\pi_{y_{1},y_{2}}
will change the empirical density, that is the perturbation in the third line. We should also remark that such perturbation will vanish when
M\notin\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})
, since there is a regularization of density
\rho\mapsto\rho\vee\varepsilon\wedge(1-\varepsilon)
in our definition (
6.34
).

The case
\eta_{y_{1}}=0,\eta_{y_{2}}=1
is similar. As the indicator
\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\eta_{y_{1}}=1,\eta_{y_{2}}=0\right\}}
is independent of the other terms in the last line under
\mathbb{P}_{\rho}
, we obtain

(6.52)

\left\langle{c_{y_{1},y_{2}}(\pi_{y_{1},y_{2}}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}\\
\leqslant 2\lambda\chi(\rho)\left(1+\sum_{M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})}\mathbb{P}_{\rho}\left[\sum_{x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-},x\neq y_{1}}\eta_{x}=M\right]\left\langle{\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}\right),

where we define
\Delta_{M,z}
to simplify the notation

(6.53)

\displaystyle\Delta_{M,z}:=\phi_{\frac{M+1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}-\phi_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi},\qquad M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}).

Because there are
3^{(d-1)m}
terms like (
6.52
) from boundary layer
b\in({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})^{*}
and the normalization factor is
\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}
, our object is to show that each term above is of order
O(3^{Kn+sm})
with some
s\in(0,1)
and
K\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
. Then roughly we get an estimate

(6.54)

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}\leqslant\sum_{b\in({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})^{*}}\frac{1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left\langle{\frac{1}{2}c_{b}(\pi_{b}(\ell_{\xi}+\hat{\phi}^{(\varepsilon)}_{m,n,\xi}))^{2}}\right\rangle_{\rho}\leqslant O(3^{Kn+(s-1)m}).

By a careful choice of
n\ll m
, we can make the contribution from
\mathbf{III}
small.

To obtain the estimate above, we also need to treat the spatial cancellation in
\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}
. Thus, we develop it as

\left\langle{\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n},z\neq z^{\prime}\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n},\dist(z^{\prime},\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})>3^{n}\end{subarray}}\left\langle{\Delta_{M,z}\Delta_{M,z^{\prime}}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}.

We treat the diagonal terms and off-diagonal terms separately.

Step 3.1: the diagonal term
\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}
.
For this term, we use the local equivalence of ensembles from (
6.18
)

(6.55)

\displaystyle\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}

\displaystyle\leqslant(1+4\cdot 3^{d(2n-m)})\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}|-1}}.

Assuming that
n<\frac{m}{2}
as (
6.41
), the factor
(1+4\cdot 3^{d(2n-m)})
is smaller than
2
.
To simplify the notation, we introduce the following shorthand expression

(6.56)

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}:=\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\qquad\hat{\rho}^{\prime}:=\frac{M+1}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|},\qquad\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}:=\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|-1}.

Then for
M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-})
, as the difference between
\hat{\rho},\hat{\rho}^{\prime},\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}
is roughly
3^{-dm}
and the three terms are not degenerate at
0
or
1
, we have an estimate for the two-sided bias factor of (
6.6
)

(6.57)

\displaystyle\forall M\in\mathcal{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}),\qquad 1\leqslant\widetilde{\Theta}_{\hat{\rho},\hat{\rho}^{\prime}},\widetilde{\Theta}_{\hat{\rho},\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}}\leqslant 1+3^{-dm}\varepsilon^{-1}.

Insert the notation (
6.56
) to (
6.53
), the quantity
\Delta_{M,z}
has a clear expression

(6.58)

\displaystyle\Delta_{M,z}=\phi_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}-\phi_{\hat{\rho},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi},

and
\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|-1}}
is just the expectation under the grand canonical ensemble of density
\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}
for difference between the correctors of densities
\hat{\rho}
and
\hat{\rho}^{\prime}

(6.59)

\displaystyle\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\frac{M}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}^{-}|-1}}=\left\langle{(\phi_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi}-\phi_{\hat{\rho},z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},\xi})^{2}}\right\rangle_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}}.

Therefore, we use the continuity in density (
6.11
) and (
6.57
)

(6.60)

\begin{split}\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}^{-}_{m}\setminus\{y_{1}\},M}&\leqslant 2\left\langle{\left(\Delta_{M,z}\right)^{2}}\right\rangle_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}}\\
&\leqslant 10\lambda\cdot 3^{(d+2)n}\left(\widetilde{\Theta}_{\hat{\rho},\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}+\widetilde{\Theta}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},\hat{\rho}^{\prime\prime}}^{(3^{n}+2\mathbf{r})^{d}}-2\right)\\
&\leqslant 20\lambda\cdot 3^{(d+2)n}\cdot\left(\left(1+3^{-dm}\varepsilon^{-1}\right)^{3^{dn}}-1\right)\\
&\leqslant 20\lambda\cdot 3^{(2d+2)n}\cdot 3^{-dm}\cdot\varepsilon^{-1}.\end{split}

Here we also need to add an assumption

(6.61)

\displaystyle 3^{dn}\ll 3^{dm}\cdot\varepsilon.
